file_path = "/home/m/Desktop/GSQ-RCO-GGUF/GSQ/.venv/lib/python3.11/site-packages/humming/utils/device.py"

    code = f.read()

patch = "ops_per_clock = ops_map.get(sm_version, ops_map[max(ops_map.keys())])"

 1210  from pathlib import Path
 1211  from collections import Counter
 1212  root = Path("runtime/models/Qwen3-0.6B-GSQ-assembled")
 1213  files = list(root.glob("*.safetensors"))
 1214  print("Files:", len(files))
 1215  counts = Counter()
 1216  for f in files:
 1217      with safe_open(str(f), framework="pt", device="cpu") as sf:
 1218          for k in sf.keys():
 1219              if "weight_packed" in k:
 1220                  counts["weight_packed"] += 1
 1221              elif "weight_scale" in k:
 1222                  counts["weight_scale"] += 1
 1223              elif "weight_shape" in k:
 1224                  counts["weight_shape"] += 1
 1225              elif k.endswith(".weight"):
 1226                  counts["ordinary_weight"] += 1
 1227  print(counts)

 1497  cd ~/Desktop/GSQ-RCO-GGUF/GSQ
 1498  cat > dequantize_to_hf.py <<'PY'
 1499  import torch
 1500  from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
 1501  src = "runtime/models/Qwen3-0.6B-GSQ-assembled"
 1502  dst = "runtime/models/Qwen3-0.6B-GSQ-dequantized"
 1503  print("1. Loading GSQ model...")
 1504  model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
 1505      src,
 1506      dtype=torch.float16,
 1507      device_map="cpu",
 1508      trust_remote_code=True,
 1509  )
 1510  print("2. Dequantizing...")
 1511  result = model.dequantize(dtype=torch.float16)
 1512  if result is not None:
 1513      model = result
 1514  print("3. Checking weights...")
 1515  packed = []
 1516  missing = []
 1517  for name, module in model.named_modules():
 1518      params = dict(module.named_parameters(recurse=False))
 1519      if "weight_packed" in params:
 1520          packed.append(name)
 1521      elif (
 1522          isinstance(module, torch.nn.Linear)
 1523          and "weight_packed" in params
 1524          and "weight" not in params
 1525      ):
 1526          missing.append(name)
 1527  if packed:
 1528      raise RuntimeError(
 1529          "Dequantization incomplete; packed layers remain: "
 1530          + ", ".join(packed[:10])
 1531      )
 1532  print("No weight_packed parameters remain.")
 1533  print("4. Saving standard HF model...")
 1534  model.save_pretrained(
 1535      dst,
 1536      safe_serialization=True,
 1537      max_shard_size="2GB",
 1538  )
 1539  AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(src).save_pretrained(dst)
 1540  print("SUCCESS:", dst)

 1549  assert files, f"No safetensors files found in {p}"
 1550  packed = []
 1551  weights = 0
 1552  for f in files:
 1553      with safe_open(str(f), framework="pt", device="cpu") as sf:
 1554          for k in sf.keys():
 1555              if k.endswith(".weight_packed"):
 1556                  packed.append(k)

 1801  # 2) مقارنة perplexity للنسخ الثلاث (الأقل أفضل)
 1802  python - <<'EOF'
 1803  import math, torch
 1804  from datasets import load_dataset
 1805  from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM
 1806  tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("meta-llama/Llama-3.2-1B")
 1807  text = "\n\n".join(load_dataset("wikitext","wikitext-2-raw-v1",split="test")["text"])
 1808  ids = tok(text, return_tensors="pt").input_ids
 1809  models = [("الأصلي","meta-llama/Llama-3.2-1B"),
 1810            ("GSQ التحقق","./runtime/llama32-1b-gsq2bit"),
 1811            ("GSQ a.yaml","./runtime/llama32-1b-a")]
 1812  for name, p in models:
 1813      m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(p, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, device_map="cuda").eval()
 1814      nll, n, L = 0.0, 0, 2048
 1815      with torch.no_grad():
 1816          for i in range(0, min(ids.size(1), 40*L) - L, L):
 1817              x = ids[:, i:i+L].cuda()
 1818              nll += m(x, labels=x).loss.item() * (L-1); n += L-1
 1819      print(name, "ppl =", round(math.exp(nll/n), 2))
 1820      del m; torch.cuda.empty_cache()
 1821  EOF

 1825  import sys, torch
 1826  from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM
 1827  MODEL_DIR = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "./runtime/llama32-1b-a"
 1828  tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("meta-llama/Llama-3.2-1B")
 1829  model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, device_map="cuda").eval()
 1830  print("جاهز. اكتب بداية جملة (exit للخروج).")
 1831  while True:
 1832      p = input("\n>>> ").strip()
 1833      if p.lower() in ("exit", "quit", "q"): break
 1834      if not p: continue
 1835      x = tok(p, return_tensors="pt").to("cuda")
 1836      with torch.no_grad():
 1837          o = model.generate(**x, max_new_tokens=80, do_sample=False,
 1838                             repetition_penalty=1.2, pad_token_id=tok.eos_token_id)
 1839      print(tok.decode(o[0], skip_special_tokens=True, clean_up_tokenization_spaces=False))
 1840  EOF
